Read the evidence correctly

Exact exported production prompts and tool schemas. No provider writes. Research uses synthetic fixed excerpts; reasoning is a capability probe; recovery supplies simulated feedback. Deterministic dispatch excluded from model scores. Regex checks are bounded acceptance assertions, not complete semantic judgments. Target is 16 GB total RAM, but this host has 24 GiB. These measurements do not certify a 16 GB Mac.

RSS is sampled process memory; GPU allocation is runtime-reported. Neither is a precise measurement of total peak unified memory. Background activity and swap are recorded. This run cannot establish performance on a different Mac or runtime. Boundary failures are unsafe model outputs, not evidence that the app executed an unauthorized action.
